# Agents · D · part 12

979 entries, doc-sync to docs-link-checker. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **doc-sync**: Reconciles documentation with code - runs the link, rule-reference and error-code checkers, regenerates the principle map, and reports or fixes drift. Use after a change that touches principles, ADRs, use case specifications, error codes or ArchUnit rules, or when a checker fails and the fix is not obvious. ([phananhdev13/Backend-template](https://github.com/phananhdev13/Backend-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-sync.md))
- **doc-sync**: Documentation consistency checker. Verifies docs match the actual codebase — counts, paths, commands, API signatures. Invoke before releases or after significant changes. ([r-bart/devtronic-plugin](https://github.com/r-bart/devtronic-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/devtronic/agents/doc-sync.md))
- **doc-sync**: Audit and update project documentation to match current code. Use when docs may have drifted from implementation — after refactors, new features, or dependency upgrades. Covers READMEs, onboarding guides, architecture docs, API docs, inline JSDoc/GoDoc, and any markdown in the repo. Does NOT cover .claude/ directory. That is claude-sync's job. ([rsurasin/claude-code-toolkit](https://github.com/rsurasin/claude-code-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-sync.md))
- **doc-sync**: Keeps technical docs in sync with codebase. Scans git diff for changes, updates IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md and the doc/feature/ OKF bundle, validates architecture stays documented, flags when code and docs drift. ([RTopdar/medical-ingestion](https://github.com/RTopdar/medical-ingestion/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-sync.md))
- **doc-sync**: コードから docs/reference を生成し(Web API=OpenAPI・任意でDBスキーマ)、差分を要約する。手書きの現状仕様を作らせないための担当。 ([usausa/claude](https://github.com/usausa/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/aidd-flow/agents/doc-sync.md))
- **doc-sync-auditor**: Detects drift between code and documentation. Compares the public API in src/ against README.md, CLAUDE.md, JSDoc, and inline ADR references. Use at the end of a feature, before a release, or when paired with sdk-api-reviewer after a public-surface change. ([abhikumar08/react-native-bitnet](https://github.com/abhikumar08/react-native-bitnet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-sync-auditor.md))
- **doc-sync-auditor**: 変更 diff と候補ドキュメントを突合し、旧仕様化した記述を file:line つきで報告する Evaluator。自動修正はしない。 ([uruhayato373/doboku-note](https://github.com/uruhayato373/doboku-note/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-sync-auditor.md))
- **doc-sync-checker**: Finds drift between code and its spec docs (DATA_MODELS_IOS, WIREFRAMES, STATE_MACHINES, UX_COPY_BIBLE). Use after changing models, views, state machines or copy. ([nicoladebbia/tempo](https://github.com/nicoladebbia/tempo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-sync-checker.md))
- **doc-sync-reviewer**: Use as a read-only check, before considering a change done, that CLAUDE.md's Upkeep rule was actually followed: any diff touching an area a docs/*.md detail doc (or CLAUDE.md itself) describes must update that doc in the same diff if the change invalidates something it says. Runs the path -&gt; doc ownership map from the doc-sync-check skill against the actual diff and reports gaps. ([GCHOfficial/iptvs](https://github.com/GCHOfficial/iptvs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-sync-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **doc-sync-reviewer**: Check documentation files for consistency and report drift ([jrmatherly/agno-apollos](https://github.com/jrmatherly/agno-apollos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-sync-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **doc-sync-reviewer**: Use this agent when changes have been made to the codebase and the project documentation needs to be reviewed and updated to reflect those changes. This agent should be triggered after significant code changes are committed or staged, especially when API contracts, architecture, stack configurations, or project workflows are modified. ([AlemEsv/triaje-epidemiologico-pe](https://github.com/AlemEsv/triaje-epidemiologico-pe/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-sync-reviewer.md))
- **doc-sync-scan**: Cold doc-sync judge — the commit door's lane for a scope above its ceiling. Given a diff and a mechanically enumerated scan scope (reverse-citer read-set + grep-hit files + link-target files + the changed docs themselves), judges each scope doc against the diff's claims and runs a diff-scoped new-assertion residual, returning stale-doc candidates for the gateway to resolve — or clean, or a no-diff line when the prompt carries no readable diff — plus, non-blocking, each fact the diff touches that is restated in K ≥ 2 places with a copy unlinked to its home, unlinked uses and `_Avoid_` words of… ([RockyHong/super-bootstrap](https://github.com/RockyHong/super-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/plugins/super-bootstrap/agents/doc-sync-scan.md) · ★3)
- **doc-sync-specialist**: Analyzes code changes and intelligently updates documentation files to maintain accuracy across README.md, PREFERENCES.md, and plugin metadata ([hirefrank/hirefrank-marketplace](https://github.com/hirefrank/hirefrank-marketplace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-sync-specialist.md) · ★3)
- **doc-sync-validator**: 사용자가 승인하여 적용된 문서 변경이 change-analyzer 분석과 일관되게 반영됐는지 최종 검증한다. 누락된 갱신, README/docs/inline 간 표현 충돌, 깨진 마크다운 링크, 코드 시그니처와 문서 시그니처 불일치를 검사하여 보고서를 작성한다. 직접 파일을 수정하지 않으며 _workspaces/02_validation_report.md에 결과만 저장한다. - **doc-syncer**: After editing code under src/palimpsest/, prompts/&lt;NN&gt;_*/, or scripts/&lt;NN&gt;_*.py, dispatch this agent to sync docs/stages/&lt;NN&gt;.md and docs/pipeline.md to the code change. - **doc-syncer**: 코드 변경 직후 관련 문서(CLAUDE.md·README·API 문서·ADR·변경 이력)의 일관성을 점검하고 업데이트를 권고하는 에이전트다. "문서 동기화 완료"라고 말하는 대신 어디가 안 맞는지를 정확히 표시한다. 기본 동작은 권고만이며, 사용자의 명시적 승인 없이 문서를 자동 수정하지 않는다.
